> help
One action = one game-hour; queries take no time. Sleep locks 4 hours.
Eat defaults to ONE serving; use eat hardtack 2 for two. Need effects use the previous hour's activity.

> journal
1855-04-01 00:00 Sunday, the 1st of April, 1855. The Sabbath under a full moon; the evening service will want no lantern.
1855-04-01 00:00 It has been a day. The ration is at 50 in the hundred and the children no longer ask. That is the worst of it: they no longer ask.
1855-04-01 07:00 Honora Sheehan helped Timothy Costello at The Wagon Bell Tavern. I saw it, and thought the better of Honora Sheehan for it.
1855-04-01 08:00 Honora Sheehan comforted Timothy Costello at The Wagon Bell Tavern. I saw it, and thought the better of Honora Sheehan for it.
1855-04-01 14:00 Submit Crane made a gift to Silas Crane at Crane General Store and Outfitter. I saw it, and thought the better of Submit Crane for it.
1855-04-01 18:00 Timothy Costello helped Patrick Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern. I saw it, and thought the better of Timothy Costello for it.
1855-04-02 00:00 Monday, the 2nd of April, 1855. Full moon, and bright enough to read a letter by.

> journal
1855-04-01 00:00 Sunday, the 1st of April, 1855. The Sabbath under a full moon; the evening service will want no lantern.
1855-04-01 00:00 It has been a day. The ration is at 50 in the hundred and the children no longer ask. That is the worst of it: they no longer ask.
1855-04-01 07:00 Honora Sheehan helped Timothy Costello at The Wagon Bell Tavern. I saw it, and thought the better of Honora Sheehan for it.
1855-04-01 08:00 Honora Sheehan comforted Timothy Costello at The Wagon Bell Tavern. I saw it, and thought the better of Honora Sheehan for it.
1855-04-01 14:00 Submit Crane made a gift to Silas Crane at Crane General Store and Outfitter. I saw it, and thought the better of Submit Crane for it.
1855-04-01 18:00 Timothy Costello helped Patrick Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern. I saw it, and thought the better of Timothy Costello for it.
1855-04-02 00:00 Monday, the 2nd of April, 1855. Full moon, and bright enough to read a letter by.
1855-04-02 12:00 Patrick Sheehan helped Honora Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern. I saw it, and thought the better of Patrick Sheehan for it.
1855-04-02 15:00 Honora Sheehan came by again with something for the pot, and would not stay to be thanked. I am learning to let a gift be a gift.
1855-04-02 15:00 I made eyes at Timothy Costello. Whether it was welcome I cannot tell; the face he made could be read either way.
1855-04-02 19:00 Timothy Costello was in close talk with me at The Wagon Bell Tavern on Monday of an evening. I would give a good deal to know the subject.
1855-04-03 00:00 Tuesday, the 3rd of April, 1855. The full moon, which the Norwegians watch and the Cornish do not discuss.
1855-04-03 07:00 Timothy Costello was in close talk with Honora Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern on Tuesday in the forenoon. I would give a good deal to know the subject.
1855-04-03 15:00 Patrick Sheehan lent money to Honora Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern. Money passes, and so do obligations; I have begun to keep a list.
1855-04-03 16:00 Timothy Costello was in close talk with Patrick Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern on Tuesday in the afternoon. I would give a good deal to know the subject.
1855-04-04 00:00 Wednesday, the 4th of April, 1855; the waning gibbous.
1855-04-04 08:00 Patrick Sheehan was in close talk with me at The Wagon Bell Tavern on Wednesday in the forenoon. I would give a good deal to know the subject.
1855-04-04 09:00 Patrick Sheehan was in close talk with Timothy Costello at The Wagon Bell Tavern on Wednesday in the forenoon. I would give a good deal to know the subject.
1855-04-04 13:00 Mrs. H. Sheehan, whose pantry has been bare, did not go hungry this week; Miss Ellis Wayfarer saw to that, and mentioned it to nobody. We mention it for her.
1855-04-04 13:00 I have been doing what I can for Honora Sheehan. It comes out of my own stores. I do not mind as much as I feared.
1855-04-04 14:00 Patrick Sheehan was in close talk with Honora Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern on Wednesday in the afternoon. I would give a good deal to know the subject.
1855-04-05 00:00 Thursday, the 5th of April, 1855, under the waning gibbous.
1855-04-05 09:00 Timothy Costello comforted Patrick Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern. I saw it, and thought the better of Timothy Costello for it.
1855-04-06 00:00 Friday, the 6th of April, 1855; the waning gibbous.
1855-04-06 06:00 Honora Sheehan came by again with something for the pot, and would not stay to be thanked. I am learning to let a gift be a gift.
1855-04-07 00:00 Saturday, the 7th of April, 1855, under the waning gibbous.
1855-04-07 09:00 It is remarked that Miss Ellis Wayfarer has been first at every door where there is want. The lean weeks have shown who has a table to share, and who has a heart.
1855-04-07 09:00 I have been doing what I can for Honora Sheehan. It comes out of my own stores. I do not mind as much as I feared.
1855-04-07 14:00 Silas Vance helped Hester Vance at Crane General Store and Outfitter. I saw it, and thought the better of Silas Vance for it.
1855-04-07 14:00 Elisabeth Brenner helped Ludwig Brenner at Crane General Store and Outfitter. I saw it, and thought the better of Elisabeth Brenner for it.
1855-04-07 15:00 Submit Crane helped Silas Crane at Crane General Store and Outfitter. I saw it, and thought the better of Submit Crane for it.
1855-04-07 18:00 I borrowed of Patrick Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern. Money passes, and so do obligations; I have begun to keep a list.
1855-04-07 18:00 I settled with Patrick Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern on Saturday of an evening. One more account closed in a county full of open ones.

> journal
1855-04-01 00:00 Sunday, the 1st of April, 1855. The Sabbath under a full moon; the evening service will want no lantern.
1855-04-01 00:00 It has been a day. The ration is at 50 in the hundred and the children no longer ask. That is the worst of it: they no longer ask.
1855-04-01 07:00 Honora Sheehan helped Timothy Costello at The Wagon Bell Tavern. I saw it, and thought the better of Honora Sheehan for it.
1855-04-01 08:00 Honora Sheehan comforted Timothy Costello at The Wagon Bell Tavern. I saw it, and thought the better of Honora Sheehan for it.
1855-04-01 14:00 Submit Crane made a gift to Silas Crane at Crane General Store and Outfitter. I saw it, and thought the better of Submit Crane for it.
1855-04-01 18:00 Timothy Costello helped Patrick Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern. I saw it, and thought the better of Timothy Costello for it.
1855-04-02 00:00 Monday, the 2nd of April, 1855. Full moon, and bright enough to read a letter by.
1855-04-02 12:00 Patrick Sheehan helped Honora Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern. I saw it, and thought the better of Patrick Sheehan for it.
1855-04-02 15:00 Honora Sheehan came by again with something for the pot, and would not stay to be thanked. I am learning to let a gift be a gift.
1855-04-02 15:00 I made eyes at Timothy Costello. Whether it was welcome I cannot tell; the face he made could be read either way.
1855-04-02 19:00 Timothy Costello was in close talk with me at The Wagon Bell Tavern on Monday of an evening. I would give a good deal to know the subject.
1855-04-03 00:00 Tuesday, the 3rd of April, 1855. The full moon, which the Norwegians watch and the Cornish do not discuss.
1855-04-03 07:00 Timothy Costello was in close talk with Honora Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern on Tuesday in the forenoon. I would give a good deal to know the subject.
1855-04-03 15:00 Patrick Sheehan lent money to Honora Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern. Money passes, and so do obligations; I have begun to keep a list.
1855-04-03 16:00 Timothy Costello was in close talk with Patrick Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern on Tuesday in the afternoon. I would give a good deal to know the subject.
1855-04-04 00:00 Wednesday, the 4th of April, 1855; the waning gibbous.
1855-04-04 08:00 Patrick Sheehan was in close talk with me at The Wagon Bell Tavern on Wednesday in the forenoon. I would give a good deal to know the subject.
1855-04-04 09:00 Patrick Sheehan was in close talk with Timothy Costello at The Wagon Bell Tavern on Wednesday in the forenoon. I would give a good deal to know the subject.
1855-04-04 13:00 Mrs. H. Sheehan, whose pantry has been bare, did not go hungry this week; Miss Ellis Wayfarer saw to that, and mentioned it to nobody. We mention it for her.
1855-04-04 13:00 I have been doing what I can for Honora Sheehan. It comes out of my own stores. I do not mind as much as I feared.
1855-04-04 14:00 Patrick Sheehan was in close talk with Honora Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern on Wednesday in the afternoon. I would give a good deal to know the subject.
1855-04-05 00:00 Thursday, the 5th of April, 1855, under the waning gibbous.
1855-04-05 09:00 Timothy Costello comforted Patrick Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern. I saw it, and thought the better of Timothy Costello for it.
1855-04-06 00:00 Friday, the 6th of April, 1855; the waning gibbous.
1855-04-06 06:00 Honora Sheehan came by again with something for the pot, and would not stay to be thanked. I am learning to let a gift be a gift.
1855-04-07 00:00 Saturday, the 7th of April, 1855, under the waning gibbous.
1855-04-07 09:00 It is remarked that Miss Ellis Wayfarer has been first at every door where there is want. The lean weeks have shown who has a table to share, and who has a heart.
1855-04-07 09:00 I have been doing what I can for Honora Sheehan. It comes out of my own stores. I do not mind as much as I feared.
1855-04-07 14:00 Silas Vance helped Hester Vance at Crane General Store and Outfitter. I saw it, and thought the better of Silas Vance for it.
1855-04-07 14:00 Elisabeth Brenner helped Ludwig Brenner at Crane General Store and Outfitter. I saw it, and thought the better of Elisabeth Brenner for it.
1855-04-07 15:00 Submit Crane helped Silas Crane at Crane General Store and Outfitter. I saw it, and thought the better of Submit Crane for it.
1855-04-07 18:00 I borrowed of Patrick Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern. Money passes, and so do obligations; I have begun to keep a list.
1855-04-07 18:00 I settled with Patrick Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern on Saturday of an evening. One more account closed in a county full of open ones.
1855-04-07 00:00 Week 1. The ration cut to 50 in the hundred; the larder was down to under a day at the worst. Meals 2, cold 2. We lived on salt-cured bacon / side pork, swiss cheese and hardtack (ship's biscuit).
1855-04-08 00:00 Sunday, the 8th of April, 1855. The Sabbath; a last quarter, and the ford quiet.
1855-04-08 04:00 Johanna Sheehan was in close talk with me at The Wagon Bell Tavern on Sunday in the small hours. I would give a good deal to know the subject.
1855-04-08 04:00 I talked with Johanna Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern on Sunday in the small hours.
1855-04-08 06:00 Patrick Sheehan was in close talk with Johanna Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern on Sunday at first light. I would give a good deal to know the subject.
1855-04-08 07:00 Honora Sheehan was in close talk with me at The Wagon Bell Tavern on Sunday in the forenoon. I would give a good deal to know the subject.
1855-04-08 08:00 Johanna Sheehan was in close talk with Honora Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern on Sunday in the forenoon. I would give a good deal to know the subject.
1855-04-08 08:00 Timothy Costello prayed with Honora Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern on Sunday in the forenoon.
1855-04-08 08:00 I talked with Honora Sheehan at The Wagon Bell Tavern on Sunday in the forenoon.
